 }
+
+#[test]
+fn production_writer_notes_cover_seed_only_and_bootstrap_cycles() {
+    let p = Project::new();
+    let tables = "use identity password\ntable staff:\n  principal: principal\n  unique principal\n  public\n  insert <- exists staff[viewer]\n  online only\nmutation addStaff(principal: principal):\n  return insert staff { principal }\ntable catalog:\n  title: text 1..80\n  public\n  online only\n";
+    p.source(tables);
+    let out = p.run(&["check", "--json"]);
+    assert!(out.status.success(), "{out:?}");
+    let json = parsed(&out);
+    let notes: Vec<_> = json["notes"].as_array().unwrap().iter().filter(|n| n["code"] == "N_NO_PRODUCTION_WRITER").collect();
+    assert_eq!(notes.len(), 2, "{json}");
+    assert!(notes.iter().all(|n| n["rewrite"].as_str().unwrap().contains("first admin")));
+    p.source(&tables.replace("insert <- exists staff[viewer]", "insert <- exists staff[viewer] or viewer = 'service:bootstrap'"));
+    let out = p.run(&["check", "--json"]); assert!(out.status.success(), "{out:?}");
+    let json = parsed(&out);
+    assert_eq!(json["notes"].as_array().unwrap().iter().filter(|n| n["code"] == "N_NO_PRODUCTION_WRITER").count(), 1);
+}
+
+#[test]
+fn fixture_arguments_are_checked_without_execution_and_saved_values_are_unknown() {
+    let p = Project::new();
+    p.source("use personas alice\nquery answer(n: int 1..5, text: text 2..8):\n  return n\n");
+    fs::create_dir(p.0.join("fixtures")).unwrap();
+    fs::write(p.0.join("fixtures/test.cases.jsonl"), "{\"op\":\"answer\",\"args\":{\"n\":6,\"text\":\"okay\"}}\n{\"op\":\"answer\",\"args\":{\"n\":2}}\n{\"op\":\"answer\",\"args\":{\"n\":\"$earlier\",\"text\":\"okay\"}}\n").unwrap();
+    let out = p.run(&["check", "--json"]); assert!(out.status.success(), "{out:?}");
+    let json = parsed(&out);
+    let notes: Vec<_> = json["notes"].as_array().unwrap().iter().filter(|n| n["code"] == "N_FIXTURE_ARGUMENT_DRIFT").collect();
+    assert_eq!(notes.len(), 2, "{json}");
+    assert_eq!(notes[0]["site"]["line"], 1); assert_eq!(notes[1]["site"]["line"], 2);
+    assert!(!p.store().exists(), "check must not create or mutate a fixture store");
+}
+
+#[test]
+fn production_readiness_follows_positive_cycles_and_read_through_parents() {
+    let p = Project::new();
+    let notes = |source: &str| {
+        p.source(source); let out = p.run(&["check", "--json"]); assert!(out.status.success(), "{out:?}");
+        parsed(&out)["notes"].as_array().unwrap().iter().filter(|n| n["code"] == "N_NO_PRODUCTION_WRITER").map(|n| n["message"].as_str().unwrap().split(':').next().unwrap().to_owned()).collect::<Vec<_>>()
+    };
+    let cycle = "use identity password\ntable a:\n  principal: principal\n  unique principal\n  read <- allow\n  insert <- exists b[viewer]\n  online only\ntable b:\n  principal: principal\n  unique principal\n  read <- allow\n  insert <- exists a[viewer]\n  online only\nmutation addA(principal: principal):\n  return insert a { principal }\nmutation addB(principal: principal):\n  return insert b { principal }\n";
+    assert_eq!(notes(cycle), ["a", "b"]);
+    assert!(notes(&cycle.replace("insert <- exists a[viewer]", "insert <- exists a[viewer] or viewer = 'service:bootstrap'")).is_empty());
+    let through = "use identity password\ntable parents:\n  owner: principal\n  immutable owner\n  read <- .owner = viewer\n  insert <- .owner = viewer\n  online only\ntable children:\n  parent: parents\n  immutable parent\n  read through .parent\n  insert <- .parent.owner = viewer\n  online only\nmutation addChild(parent: parents):\n  return insert children { parent }\n";
+    assert_eq!(notes(through), ["children", "parents"]);
+    assert!(notes(&format!("{through}\nmutation addParent():\n  return insert parents {{ owner: viewer }}\n")).is_empty());
+}

+
+#[test]
+fn password_starters_and_expo_web_journal_follow_schema() {
+    for flag in ["--web", "--expo"] {
+        for identity in ["use identity password", "use personas alice"] {
+            let dir = Dir::new(); fs::create_dir(dir.0.join("snapback")).unwrap();
+            fs::write(dir.0.join("snapback/schema.q"), format!("{identity}\ntable notes:\n  body: text 1..80\n  public\n  online only\nmutation add(body: text 1..80):\n  return insert notes {{ body }}\n")).unwrap();
+            let out = dir.run(flag); assert!(out.status.success(), "{out:?}");
+            let app = fs::read_to_string(dir.0.join(if flag == "--expo" { "App.tsx" } else { "src/App.tsx" })).unwrap();
+            if identity.contains("password") { assert!(app.contains("auth.signup") && app.contains("auth.login") && !app.contains("auth.guest"), "{app}"); }
+            let client = fs::read_to_string(dir.0.join("src/client.ts")).unwrap();
+            assert!(!client.contains("webDevice"), "{client}");
+            assert!(client.contains("get(\"api\")") && client.contains("registerWebWitness"), "{client}");
+            if flag == "--expo" { assert!(client.contains("nativeDevice"), "{client}"); }
+            let pkg: Value = serde_json::from_str(&fs::read_to_string(dir.0.join("package.json")).unwrap()).unwrap();
+            assert!(pkg["scripts"]["build:web"].is_string());
+            if flag == "--expo" { assert!(pkg["scripts"]["build:web"].as_str().unwrap().contains("--clear")); }
+        }
+    }
+}
+
+#[test]
+fn adapt_switches_guest_password_and_persona_and_keeps_password_readme() {
+    for mode in ["--web", "--expo"] {
+        let dir = Dir::new(); assert!(dir.run(mode).status.success());
+        let path = dir.0.join("snapback/schema.q");
+        let original = fs::read_to_string(&path).unwrap();
+        for (identity, expected) in [("use identity password", "the password sign-in"), ("use personas alice", "a persona picker"), ("use identity password", "the password sign-in"), ("use identity", "the guest sign-in")] {
+            let body = original.lines().filter(|line| !line.starts_with("use identity") && !line.starts_with("use personas")).collect::<Vec<_>>().join("\n");
+            fs::write(&path, format!("{identity}\n{body}\n")).unwrap();
+            let out = dir.init(&["--adapt"]); assert!(out.status.success(), "{out:?}");
+            let text = String::from_utf8_lossy(&out.stdout); assert!(text.contains(expected), "{text}");
+            let app = fs::read_to_string(dir.0.join(if mode == "--web" { "src/App.tsx" } else { "App.tsx" })).unwrap();
+            if identity.contains("password") {
+                assert!(app.contains("auth.signup") && !app.contains("auth.guest"));
+                let readme = fs::read_to_string(dir.0.join("README.md")).unwrap();
+                assert!(!readme.contains("Choose \"Continue as a"), "{readme}");
+                assert!(readme.contains("Sign up or sign in"), "{readme}");
+            }
+        }
+    }
+}

## 8. Wave 9: production onboarding and a browser proof

**Implementer:** Codex for Charlie Cheever, 2026-10-12 (DX workstream).
The commission covers wave 9 fix list #6, #7, #10, #13, #14 and the jobs
recipe in #18. REL owns the persona-to-password deploy checklist; HAND owns
protocol negotiation and hot-activation envelope consistency.

Password identity produces signup/login forms on web and Expo, retaining the
existing session-store custody. Schema-first init and unmodified-screen adapt
select the password form; edited screens remain application-owned. Both
starters honor runtime `?api=` on web. Expo chooses the existing device-less
journal on web when no table syncs, preserving nativeDevice on native builds.
Expo `build:web` clears Metro on every export so a changed public URL is
compiled; Vite builds again from its current environment.

`check` emits advisory N_NO_PRODUCTION_WRITER for application tables lacking
an insert/upsert operation or blocked by positive bootstrap dependencies.
Insert rules and read-through parents are evaluated over a least fixed point
of potentially populated tables; jobs write as system, service-seat branches
are potential roots. Negated and unknown comparisons remain potentially
satisfiable: this is a sound conservative warning for the covered positive
fragment, not a general satisfiability proof. Native jobs are opaque and suppress
this absence analysis. A warning points to `guide deploy --section first admin`.
No rule, planner, admission or outbox changes are made. Fixture argument drift
is checked without execution with the existing wire decoder and argument
validator; saved-result references remain unknown. Neither note fails check.

`snapback4 witness web [dist]` serves an existing export on an allocated
loopback port, launches headless Chromium (SNAPBACK4_CHROMIUM overrides cache
and standard-path discovery), opens the real page with `?api=&witness=web`,
then uses its persona or sign-in UI (signup on the owned empty backend). It calls that app's authenticated SDK
client, using the first generated mutation or `--mutation` and `--args`, and
requires terminal `sent`; failed/pending/timeout are nonzero with a reason.
The app registers its client and generated API with
`registerWebWitness(app, api)` from `snapback4/witness-web`; starters include
this hook. It exists only under the explicit query flag, adds no authority,
and is callable by the page's own scripts already holding that client.
This proves exported client/auth/write integration, not the application's
composer UI or an arbitrary user journey. Missing registration refuses.
A fresh browser and an owned `dev --memory --port 0 --no-watch` make default
runs repeatable without changing the project's durable store. `--url` never
resets a caller-owned server; a deterministic id derived from principal,
operation selection and canonical arguments deduplicates repeated proof
writes under that store's receipt guarantees. Different arguments are new
intents. Credentials are supplied through a named environment variable,
never printed. Cleanup records the owned backend immediately at spawn, even before readiness;
its supervisor PID also stops it if the witness dies. It closes the browser/static
server and only the owned backend; long polls never require networkidle.

`upgrade [version]` detects npm/bun/pnpm by packageManager then lockfile,
refusing ambiguous locks. It resolves latest once, preflights core and installed
Expo adapter versions, installs that same exact pin through the manager,
preserves dependency sections and verifies resulting package pins. Native
rebuild and check/acceptance remain explicit. Package-manager failures return
nonzero; its ordinary lockfile/install recovery applies.

The wire table distinguishes raw responses from SDK Read/Write states.
Dropping a null mutation id is a serialization amendment only: a request
without id remains a fresh, non-deduplicated write. Callers mint ids for retries.
No id is fabricated after execution. Omitted ids remain omitted on error paths.

### Wave 9 review dispositions

Round 1 Muse: NOT READY, four material findings. All accepted: wrap the
pre-execution error response in null-id omission; distinguish guest/password
in adaptation state and reporting; retain password README text for Todos/Home
through adaptation; use pnpm's own save-exact/save-dev/save-optional flags.
The first fresh Sol review and the corrected revision's reviews follow under
`llp/reviews/`. Existing card budgets stay unchanged; the photo details now
link to the published client reference beside the new password recipe.

Round 1 fresh Sol: NOT READY, two material findings. Accepted: Playwright
credential-entry failures now produce a fixed diagnostic; arbitrary foreign
exception logs are withheld and known password text is removed from owned
diagnostics. A disabled-input Chromium case checks both output streams.
The wire table now includes raw loading/null-placeholder replies, and the
client's empty-list sentence names cuts, continuations and pending predictions.
Sol also verified startup SIGTERM cleanup and requested an immutable final review.

# Rules

**Snapback 2 optimizes the edit-to-verified loop — the agent's building an app on it, and ours building it.**

Speed is the constraint. Everything below exists to keep the edit-to-verified loop short
and to stop this repository accreting apparatus faster than anyone can remove it. These
rules bind `snapback2/` and the LLPs numbered 1000 and up; Snapback 1 is baselined, not
exempt, and its numbers may only fall.

Each rule is **[check]** (enforced by `caps`) or **[review]** (enforced by a human).

## Budgets — every budget is a trade, not a limit

- **5 blocking checks, 60s total.** A sixth requires deleting one, in the same PR. **[check]**
- **59 documents in the working set** (`llp/current/`). Today's count. A 60th requires
  archiving one — which is just removing the link. Snapback 2 aims to leave this at 15;
  Snapback 1's links are archived at promotion. **[check]**
- **12 documents in the foundation** (`llp/foundation/`). **[check]**
- **This file: 700 words.** If it grows, something becomes a check or stops being a rule. **[check]**
- **1,500 lines per source file, baseline 204.** Generated files exempt — and generated
  files are built, not committed; so are benchmark run records under `benchmarks/runs/`,
  which are evidence, not source. The baseline is Snapback 1's oversize count on the day
  the rule landed; it may not grow, and `snapback2/` contributes zero to it. **[check]**

## Blocking checks

`caps` · `ref-check` · `snapback2/scripts/test.sh` · `snapback2/scripts/measure.mjs --check` · `check-inventories`

## Loop shape

- **Every check reports all failures in one run.** Fail-fast is banned. **[check]**
- **Nothing blocks on anything slower than 60 seconds.** Slow verification runs on a
  schedule, attributed to the breaking commit, and files a bounded set of issues. **[review]**
- **Fix loops get 3 rounds.** Then stop: escalate to a human or descope. **[review]**

## Time budgets

Tracked every run of `snapback2/scripts/measure.mjs` (appends `snapback2/metrics/loop.jsonl`).
A regression is a P0 with a name on it.

| | budget |
|---|---|
| Incremental rebuild after a one-line edit | 15s |
| Test what you just changed | 30s |
| Blocking gate suite | 60s |
| Full build, warm cache | 5 min |
| **`snapback2 check` on a program (the agent's product latency)** | **1s** |
| **`snapback2 doctor` boot-to-ready** | **0.5s** |

## Scope

- **`NOT-DOING.md` is binding.** Moving something onto the doing-list means writing why
  and taking something off. **[review]**
- **Delete; don't deprecate.** No compat shims or migration paths before promotion. **[review]**
- **Nothing is compiled, transpiled, or generated at runtime that could be built ahead of
  time.** **[review]**
- **A spec needs an implementer and a date, or it isn't written.** **[review]**
- **Only implemented specs bind.** A document with no running code behind it is a
  proposal, and a proposal cannot block a PR or justify a check. **[review]**

## Agents

- **Agents remove apparatus freely and add none.** An agent PR that adds a check, script,
  registry, config file, or design doc needs explicit human approval saying so. **[review]**
- **2026-09-06 session exception:** the user delegated the search implementation
  decisions; one `snapback2-core/build.rs` may generate pinned Unicode tables at
  build time, alongside the existing package build. No new gate, registry,
  runtime generator, or Unicode dependency is authorized by this exception. **[review]**
- **Snapback 2 LLPs are numbered 1000 and up**; `llp-create` must be told. **[review]**
- **Snapback 4 LLPs are numbered 3000 and up** (`snapback4/`, LLP 3000); it adopts these budgets
  and loop shape as its own and keeps its scope in `snapback4/NOT-DOING.md`. **[review]**

## Shared machines

- Warm, named lanes, one per stream of work. Never clone fresh; reuse the worktree.
- **Never `git stash`.** Use a temporary commit.
- Kill only PIDs you recorded at launch. Never by name or pattern.

+        }
+    };

+//! Read-only production and fixture authoring notes; never an execution gate.
+use std::collections::BTreeSet;
+use snapback4_lang::{bind::Bound, Diagnostic, Site};
+use snapback4_core::{ir::{self, ProgramKind, ArgType}, schema::{RuleExpr, RuleTerm}, engine::validate, wire};
+use crate::project::Project;
+
+// @ref LLP 3000.011.003.000#8 — positive bootstrap dependencies form a least fixed point.
+pub(super) fn notes(project: &Project, bound: &Bound, fixture: Option<&String>) -> Vec<Diagnostic> {
+    let mut reachable = BTreeSet::new();
+    let mut system = BTreeSet::new();
+    let mut writers = BTreeSet::new();
+    for p in &bound.programs {
+        for (table, effect) in ir::write_tables(&p.program) {
+            if matches!(effect, "insert" | "upsert") {
+                writers.insert(table.clone());
+                if p.program.kind == ProgramKind::Job { system.insert(table); }
+            }
+        }
+        // Native jobs are opaque to the authoring analysis; no absence claim.
+        if p.program.native { return fixture_notes(project, bound, fixture); }
+    }
+    loop {
+        let previous = reachable.len();
+        for (name, table) in bound.schema.visible_tables() {
+            if bound.schema.maintain_for(name).is_some() { reachable.insert(name.to_owned()); continue; }
+            if !writers.contains(name) { continue; }
+            let refs_ready = table.through.as_ref().is_none_or(|through| reachable.contains(&through.parent));
+            if refs_ready && (system.contains(name) || possible(&table.rules.insert, &reachable)) { reachable.insert(name.to_owned()); }
+        }
+        if reachable.len() == previous { break; }
+    }
+    let sources = project.sources().unwrap_or_default();
+    let mut notes = Vec::new();
+    for (name, table) in bound.schema.visible_tables() {
+        if table.ephemeral.is_some() || bound.schema.maintain_for(name).is_some() || reachable.contains(name) { continue; }
+        let site = sources.iter().find_map(|(file, source)| source.lines().enumerate().find(|(_, line)| line.trim_start().starts_with(&format!("table {name}:"))).map(|(line, _)| Site::new(file, line as u32 + 1, 1)))
+            .unwrap_or_else(|| Site::new(&format!("table {name}"), 1, 1));
+        notes.push(Diagnostic::new(&site, "N_NO_PRODUCTION_WRITER",
+            format!("{name}: no production insert path can bootstrap this table; fixtures and /seed exist only in dev (an existing role/reference may be required)"),
+            "add an insert mutation with a declared service seat to bootstrap the first row; snapback4 guide deploy --section first admin").guide("deploy"));
+    }
+    notes.extend(fixture_notes(project, bound, fixture));
+    notes
+}
+fn possible(rule: &RuleExpr, rows: &BTreeSet<String>) -> bool {
+    match rule {
+        RuleExpr::Deny => false,
+        RuleExpr::Allow | RuleExpr::Public { .. } => true,
+        RuleExpr::Exists { table, .. } | RuleExpr::Created { table, .. } => rows.contains(table),
+        RuleExpr::And(items) => items.iter().all(|item| possible(item, rows)),
+        RuleExpr::Or(items) => items.iter().any(|item| possible(item, rows)),
+        RuleExpr::Eq(left, right) => {
+            if let (RuleTerm::Literal(a), RuleTerm::Literal(b)) = (left, right) { return a == b; }
+            let principal = match (left, right) { (RuleTerm::Viewer, RuleTerm::Literal(v)) | (RuleTerm::Literal(v), RuleTerm::Viewer) => v.as_str().ok(), _ => None };
+            if principal.is_some_and(|p| p.starts_with("service:")) { return true; }
+            if principal.is_some_and(|p| p.starts_with("dev:")) { return false; }
+            left.references().chain(right.references()).all(|(table, _)| rows.contains(table))
+        }
+        // Unknown predicates are potentially satisfiable: this lint is not a theorem of reachability.
+        RuleExpr::Ne(_, _) | RuleExpr::Compare(_, _, _) | RuleExpr::Not(_) => true,
+    }
+}
+fn fixture_notes(project: &Project, bound: &Bound, fixture: Option<&String>) -> Vec<Diagnostic> {
+    let path = fixture.map(std::path::PathBuf::from).unwrap_or_else(|| project.root.join("fixtures")).join(super::test_cases::FILE);
+    let Ok(text) = std::fs::read_to_string(&path) else { return vec![]; };
+    let mut notes = Vec::new();
+    for (index, line) in text.lines().enumerate() {
+        let line = line.trim();
+        if line.is_empty() || line.starts_with("//") { continue; }
+        let check = || -> Result<(), String> {
+            let case: serde_json::Value = serde_json::from_str(line).map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;
+            let op = case["op"].as_str().ok_or("case needs op")?;
+            let program = &bound.programs.iter().find(|p| p.program.name == op).ok_or_else(|| format!("unknown operation {op}"))?.program;
+            let empty = serde_json::json!({});
+            let args = case.get("args").unwrap_or(&empty).as_object().ok_or("args must be an object")?;
+            for (name, kind) in &program.args {
+                if !args.contains_key(name) && !kind.is_optional() && !matches!(kind, ArgType::Cursor) { return Err(format!("{op}: missing argument {name}")); }
+            }
+            for (name, value) in args {
+                let kind = program.args.get(name).ok_or_else(|| format!("{op} takes no argument {name}"))?;
+                // Saved answers are runtime values; never claim a type failure for one.
+                if saved(value) { continue; }
+                let value = wire::from_json(kind, value).map_err(|e| format!("{name}: {e}"))?;
+                validate::validate_arg(&bound.schema, kind, &value, name).map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;
+            }
+            Ok(())
+        };
+        if let Err(reason) = check() {
+            notes.push(Diagnostic::new(&Site::new(&path.to_string_lossy(), index as u32 + 1, 1), "N_FIXTURE_ARGUMENT_DRIFT", reason,
+                "update this case's arguments to the operation's current signature; run snapback4 test").guide("testing"));
+        }
+    }
+    notes
+}
+fn saved(value: &serde_json::Value) -> bool {
+    match value { serde_json::Value::String(s) => s.starts_with('$') && s.len() > 1, serde_json::Value::Array(items) => items.iter().any(saved), serde_json::Value::Object(items) => items.values().any(saved), _ => false }
+}

//! Run the packaged web witness with this exact native device binary.
use std::{path::PathBuf, process::Command};

pub(super) fn run(args: &[String]) -> i32 {
    let execute = || -> Result<i32, String> {
        let binary = std::env::current_exe().map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;
        let mut candidates = Vec::<PathBuf>::new();
        if let Some(path) = std::env::var_os("SNAPBACK4_WEB_HELPER") { candidates.push(path.into()); }
        // Installed platform package beside the client, then source checkout.
        for ancestor in binary.ancestors().skip(1) {
            candidates.push(ancestor.join("snapback4/bin/witness-web.js"));
            candidates.push(ancestor.join("packages/snapback4/bin/witness-web.js"));
        }
        let helper = candidates.into_iter().find(|p| p.is_file())
            .ok_or("install the matching snapback4 client package to run witness web")?;
        let mut command = Command::new(crate::project::node());
        command.arg(helper).args(args).env("SNAPBACK4_BIN", binary);
        if !args.iter().any(|arg| arg == "--help") {
            if let Some(project) = std::env::current_dir().ok().and_then(|cwd| crate::project::Project::locate(&cwd)) {
                let bound = project.compile().map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;
                if !bound.diagnostics.is_empty() { return Err("schema does not compile; run snapback4 check".into()); }
                let plan: Vec<_> = bound.programs.iter().filter(|p| p.program.kind == snapback4_core::ir::ProgramKind::Mutation)
                    .map(|p| serde_json::json!({ "name": p.program.name, "args": p.program.args.iter().map(|(name, kind)| (name.clone(), sample(kind))).collect::<std::collections::BTreeMap<_, _>>() })).collect();
                command.env("SNAPBACK4_WEB_PLAN", serde_json::to_string(&plan).unwrap());
            }
        }
        #[cfg(unix)] {
            use std::os::unix::process::CommandExt;
            Err(command.exec().to_string())
        }
        #[cfg(not(unix))] {
            command.status().map(|s| s.code().unwrap_or(1)).map_err(|e| e.to_string())
        }
    };
    match execute() { Ok(code) => code, Err(e) => { eprintln!("E_WITNESS_PROCESS: {e}"); 1 } }
}

fn sample(kind: &snapback4_core::ir::ArgType) -> serde_json::Value {
    use snapback4_core::ir::ArgType;
    use serde_json::{Value, json};
    match kind {
        ArgType::Optional(_) | ArgType::Cursor => Value::Null,
        ArgType::Text { min, max, format } => json!(match format {
            Some(snapback4_core::schema::TextFormat::Url) => "https://example.test/".to_owned(),
            Some(snapback4_core::schema::TextFormat::Handle) => "a".repeat((*min).max(3).min(*max) as usize),
            None => "a".repeat((*min).max(1).min(*max) as usize),
        }),
        ArgType::Bool => json!(false),
        ArgType::Int | ArgType::Time => json!(0),
        ArgType::IntRange { min, max } => json!(0i64.clamp(*min, *max)),
        ArgType::Enum { variants } => json!(variants.first()),
        ArgType::Principal => json!({ "witnessViewer": true }),
        _ => json!({ "witnessArgumentRequired": true }),
    }
}
#!/usr/bin/env node
// @ref LLP 3000.011.003.000#8 — resolve once, pin core and adapters to that same release.
import { readFileSync, existsSync } from 'node:fs';
import { dirname, join } from 'node:path';
import { spawnSync } from 'node:child_process';

function run() {
  const args = process.argv.slice(2);
  if (args.length === 1 && args[0] === '--help') { console.log('usage: snapback4 upgrade [version]\nUpgrade snapback4 and every installed snapback4-expo adapter with npm, bun or pnpm; keep exact pins. Default: latest. Rebuild native apps after changing the adapter.'); return; }
  if (args.length > 1 || (args[0] && !/^(latest|\d+\.\d+\.\d+(?:-[0-9A-Za-z.-]+)?)$/.test(args[0]))) throw Error('E_INPUT: upgrade takes an exact version or latest');
  let root = process.cwd();
  while (!existsSync(join(root, 'package.json'))) { const parent = dirname(root); if (parent === root) throw Error('E_UPGRADE: no project package.json'); root = parent; }
  const pkg = JSON.parse(readFileSync(join(root, 'package.json'), 'utf8'));
  const declared = pkg.packageManager?.split('@')[0];
  const locks = [['bun', ['bun.lock', 'bun.lockb']], ['pnpm', ['pnpm-lock.yaml']], ['npm', ['package-lock.json', 'npm-shrinkwrap.json']]].filter(([, files]) => files.some(file => existsSync(join(root, file)))).map(([pm]) => pm);
  if (declared && !['npm', 'bun', 'pnpm'].includes(declared)) throw Error('E_UPGRADE: supported package managers are npm, bun and pnpm');
  if (!declared && locks.length > 1) throw Error('E_UPGRADE: multiple package-manager lockfiles; set packageManager in package.json');
  const pm = declared ?? locks[0] ?? 'npm';
  const dependencies = ['dependencies', 'devDependencies', 'optionalDependencies'];
  const targets = ['snapback4', 'snapback4-expo'].filter(name => name === 'snapback4' || dependencies.some(key => pkg[key]?.[name]) || existsSync(join(root, 'node_modules', name, 'package.json')));
  const invoke = (command, capture = false) => {
    const result = spawnSync(pm, command, { cwd: root, encoding: 'utf8', stdio: capture ? ['ignore', 'pipe', 'pipe'] : 'inherit' });
    if (result.error || result.status !== 0) throw Error(`E_UPGRADE: ${pm} ${command[0]} failed; check registry/tool availability`);
    return result.stdout;
  };
  let version = args[0] ?? 'latest';
  if (version === 'latest') {
    const text = invoke(pm === 'bun' ? ['info', 'snapback4@latest', 'version', '--json'] : ['view', 'snapback4@latest', 'version', '--json'], true).trim();
    try { const data = JSON.parse(text); version = typeof data === 'string' ? data : data.version; }
    catch { throw Error('E_UPGRADE: package manager returned no exact core version'); }
  }
  if (typeof version !== 'string' || !/^\d+\.\d+\.\d+(?:-[0-9A-Za-z.-]+)?$/.test(version)) throw Error('E_UPGRADE: registry returned no exact core version');
  // Check the adapter release before any install changes package.json.
  for (const name of targets) invoke(pm === 'bun' ? ['info', `${name}@${version}`, 'version', '--json'] : ['view', `${name}@${version}`, 'version', '--json'], true);
  // Preserve each dependency section. One resolved version, even across sections.
  const groups = new Map();
  for (const name of targets) {
    const sections = dependencies.filter(key => pkg[key]?.[name]);
    if (sections.length > 1) throw Error(`E_UPGRADE: ${name} occurs in multiple dependency sections; consolidate it first`);
    const section = sections[0] ?? 'dependencies';
    if (!groups.has(section)) groups.set(section, []);
    groups.get(section).push(`${name}@${version}`);
  }
  for (const [section, packages] of groups) {
    const mode = section === 'devDependencies' ? (pm === 'bun' ? '--dev' : '--save-dev') : section === 'optionalDependencies' ? (pm === 'bun' ? '--optional' : '--save-optional') : null;
    invoke([pm === 'npm' ? 'install' : 'add', pm === 'bun' ? '--exact' : '--save-exact', ...(mode ? [mode] : []), ...packages]);
  }
  const after = JSON.parse(readFileSync(join(root, 'package.json'), 'utf8'));
  for (const name of targets) if (!dependencies.some(key => after[key]?.[name] === version)) throw Error(`E_UPGRADE: ${name} was not pinned to ${version}`);
  console.log(`upgrade: ${targets.join(', ')} pinned to ${version} with ${pm}; commit the lockfile, run check and acceptance tests${targets.includes('snapback4-expo') ? ', and rebuild the native app' : ''}`);
}
try { run(); } catch (error) { console.error(error.message); process.exitCode = 1; }

+  if (auth.state === "signed-in") return <>Signed in as {auth.viewer}</>;

+## Catch-up sweep after downtime
+
+Missed boundaries coalesce into one run on return, not one per missed period.
+A period-bounded job can miss older due rows. Sweep durable unfinished work
+through the latest boundary, with immutable cursor columns and a completion
+marker inside the loop:
+
+```quarry
+table embargoes:
+  dueAt: time
+  released: bool default false
+  immutable dueAt
+  public
+  online only
+job releaseDue(periodStart: time, periodEnd: time):
+  for row in embargoes where .dueAt < periodEnd by .dueAt:
+    if not row.released:
+      update embargoes[row.id] { released: true }
+schedule catchUp every 1h: releaseDue
+```
+
+No lower period bound: the first run after days offline catches every overdue
+row. Marker and chunk cursor commit together; repeated sweeps are no-ops for
+completed rows. For external work, emit in the same guarded update and use the
+effect idempotency key. The marker means queued, not external success.
+`snapback4 run releaseDue '{"periodStart":0,"periodEnd":1791763200000}'`
+queues a repair sweep. Test across several skipped periods, checking rows
+before periodStart as well as inside the latest period.
+
 ## Refusals
 
 N_CLOCK_STORE_AHEAD means recent commit stamps are more than five seconds ahead

+### Refusal order
+
+| Stage (in execution order) | Refusal |
+|---|---|
+| Argument names, wire types and declared bounds | `E_INPUT`; before program statements |
+| Each `!` lookup as reached | Hidden/missing: `NOT_FOUND` (or written `else`); later lines do not run |
+| Each `require` as reached | Its written code; lookups and requires run in source order |
+| Write target and table write rule | Missing/hidden refused target: `NOT_FOUND`; readable refused target: `E_RULE` |
+| Unique, then cap, then immutable | Written unique code or `E_CONSTRAINT`; hidden unique clash: `E_RULE`; cap: `E_CONSTRAINT` (hidden sequence occupant: `E_RULE`) |
+| After the final statement | `after insert` obligation: `E_CONSTRAINT` |
+

 It is an upsert by `id`: a reseed updates mutable columns, and changing an `immutable` one refuses
 `E_CONSTRAINT` (use a new id, `POST /dev/reset`, or restart with `--memory`). Personas, `/seed` and `/dev/reset` exist only under `dev`,
 and a persona must be declared in `use personas` (an outsider is 401 `E_AUTH`).
+
+## Browser proof: the exported app writes
+
+Run `npm run build:web`, then `snapback4 witness web dist --as alice --mutation add --args '{"title":"Browser proof"}'`.
+Password accounts: `--email proof@example.test --password-env PROOF_PASSWORD`
+(sign up first on an existing backend); `--url URL` selects that backend. Without it the
+witness owns a fresh `dev --memory` and uses the signup form for password accounts. Set `SNAPBACK4_CHROMIUM` if Chromium is
+not found. Starters register their SDK client; custom apps call
+`registerWebWitness(app, api)` from `snapback4/witness-web`, active only under
+`?witness=web`. This proves exported app/authenticated SDK integration; test
+its composer UI separately. Fresh browser/storage and a fresh default backend
+make runs repeatable; `--url` repeats use a stable write id, never reset.
+
+Never wait for `networkidle`: live pages hold a long poll. Navigate with
+`domcontentloaded`, wait for the control, and assert `sent` before cross-device
+reads. A predicted row or `pending` is not a server commit. Spawn servers
+directly via `resolveBinary()` or `node_modules/.bin/vite`, never via `npx`;
+stop only your children (recipe above). Rebuild the Expo web export when
+`EXPO_PUBLIC_SNAPBACK_URL` changes: starter `build:web` uses
+`expo export --platform web --clear`. Both starters accept runtime `?api=URL`.
+
+## Authoring and proof refusals
+
+N_FIXTURE_ARGUMENT_DRIFT means a fixture case no longer fits its operation signature. Update its arguments and run snapback4 test.
+
+E_WITNESS_BROWSER means no usable headless Chromium executable. Set SNAPBACK4_CHROMIUM.
+
+E_WITNESS_AUTH means the exported app did not sign in. Check the persona or credentials and register its client.
+
+E_WITNESS_MUTATION means no matching mutation was registered. Pass --mutation with a generated API name.
+
+E_WITNESS_ARGUMENT means an argument needs application values. Pass --args with valid values.
+
+E_WITNESS_EXPORT means the export has no index.html. Run build:web first.
+
+E_WITNESS_READY means the app could not open its write custody. Repair the reported schema or storage refusal.
+
+E_WITNESS_SENT means the write failed, stayed pending or timed out. Inspect the refusal; pending is not success.
+
+E_WITNESS_WEB means the browser proof could not complete. Check the export, browser and application.

 comes from `snapback4 token --service <name>`. Send one credential, not both.
 
+## Wire and client envelopes
+
+| Call | Success | Refusal / pending |
+|---|---|---|
+| `POST /m/<op>` | `{state:"sent", id?, seq, result, generation, store_id, head}`; inserted row: `result.id` | `{state:"failed", id?, why, current?, store_id, head}`; decode/auth/unknown-op failures can be `{denied}` |
+| `POST /q/<op>` | `{data, complete, cut, next, seq, tables, viewer, generation, store_id}`; list, row, shape or scalar | `{denied, complete:false, …}` or `{loading:true,data:null,complete:false,…}`; loading data is a placeholder, never an empty answer |
+| `POST /assets` raw bytes | `{asset:{id,type,bytes,width?,height?,duration?,name?}}`; `?as=file&name=…` for files | `{denied}`; pass `{id:asset.id}` to the mutation |
+| `await client.query(api.op, args)` | `Read<T>`: `{data, existence, complete, …}`; narrow with `isReady` | `{denied}`; one-shot reads settle or refuse; observers can also load |
+| `await client.mutate(api.op, args)` | `Write<T>`: `{state:"sent", id, seq, result?, …}` | `{state:"failed", id, why, …}` or `{state:"pending", id, …}`; pending is not success |
+
+SDK envelopes differ from raw HTTP. Check `loading` and `denied` before `data`; HTTP status alone is insufficient.
+An omitted mutation request id is omitted from its reply, never the inserted
+row id. Send an id once per intended write to retry safely; SDK clients mint
+it. Replays omit historical result/current; see the Operations contract below.
+

 **Constructor compatibility:** ready `Read<T>` values now require `existence`. index 0000000000..235a952a32
--- /dev/null
+++ b/snapback4/crates/snapback4/src/verbs/upgrade.rs
@@ -0,0 +1,16 @@
+//! Upgrade is package-manager work, run by the matching installed client helper.
+use std::{path::PathBuf, process::Command};
+pub fn run(args: &[String]) -> i32 {
+    let execute = || -> Result<i32, String> {
+        let binary = std::env::current_exe().map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;
+        let mut candidates = Vec::<PathBuf>::new();
+        if let Some(path) = std::env::var_os("SNAPBACK4_UPGRADE_HELPER") { candidates.push(path.into()); }
+        for ancestor in binary.ancestors().skip(1) {
+            candidates.push(ancestor.join("snapback4/bin/upgrade.js"));
+            candidates.push(ancestor.join("packages/snapback4/bin/upgrade.js"));
+        }
+        let helper = candidates.into_iter().find(|p| p.is_file()).ok_or("install the matching snapback4 package to run upgrade")?;
+        Command::new(crate::project::node()).arg(helper).args(args).status().map(|s| s.code().unwrap_or(1)).map_err(|e| e.to_string())
+    };
+    match execute() { Ok(code) => code, Err(e) => { eprintln!("E_UPGRADE: {e}"); 1 } }
+}

use super::*;

#[test]
fn jobs_catch_up_sweep_includes_rows_before_the_latest_period_and_repeats() {
    let source = quarry(card("jobs").unwrap()).into_iter().find(|block| block.contains("job releaseDue(")).unwrap();
    let bound = snapback4_lang::compile(&[("schema.q".into(), source)]);
    assert!(bound.diagnostics.is_empty(), "{:?}", bound.diagnostics);
    let mut engine = Engine::new(snapback4_core::Store::memory().unwrap(), bound.schema, bound.programs.into_iter().map(|p| p.program)).unwrap();
    engine.seed([100, 500, 2000].into_iter().enumerate().map(|(n, due)| ("embargoes".into(), Value::Row(BTreeMap::from([
        ("id".into(), id(&format!("due{n}"))), ("dueAt".into(), Value::Int(due)), ("released".into(), Value::Bool(false)),
    ]).into())))).unwrap();
    for _ in 0..2 {
        let run = engine.run_job("releaseDue", args(&[("periodStart", Value::Int(400)), ("periodEnd", Value::Int(1000))]), 1000).unwrap();
        assert_eq!(run.state, JobState::Done, "{run:?}");
        let rows = engine.store().rows("embargoes").unwrap();
        for row in rows { assert_eq!(row.field("released").unwrap(), &Value::Bool(row.field("dueAt").unwrap().as_int().unwrap() < 1000)); }
    }
}

+#[test]
+fn production_writer_notes_cover_seed_only_and_bootstrap_cycles() {
+    let p = Project::new();
+    let tables = "use identity password\ntable staff:\n  principal: principal\n  unique principal\n  public\n  insert <- exists staff[viewer]\n  online only\nmutation addStaff(principal: principal):\n  return insert staff { principal }\ntable catalog:\n  title: text 1..80\n  public\n  online only\n";

+    let json = parsed(&out);
+    let notes: Vec<_> = json["notes"].as_array().unwrap().iter().filter(|n| n["code"] == "N_NO_PRODUCTION_WRITER").collect();
+    assert_eq!(notes.len(), 2, "{json}");
+    assert!(notes.iter().all(|n| n["rewrite"].as_str().unwrap().contains("first admin")));
+    p.source(&tables.replace("insert <- exists staff[viewer]", "insert <- exists staff[viewer] or viewer = 'service:bootstrap'"));
+    let out = p.run(&["check", "--json"]); assert!(out.status.success(), "{out:?}");
+    let json = parsed(&out);
+    assert_eq!(json["notes"].as_array().unwrap().iter().filter(|n| n["code"] == "N_NO_PRODUCTION_WRITER").count(), 1);
+}

+    let out = p.run(&["check", "--json"]); assert!(out.status.success(), "{out:?}");

+    assert_eq!(notes.len(), 2, "{json}");

+#[test]
+fn production_readiness_follows_positive_cycles_and_read_through_parents() {
+    let p = Project::new();
+    let notes = |source: &str| {
+        p.source(source); let out = p.run(&["check", "--json"]); assert!(out.status.success(), "{out:?}");
+        parsed(&out)["notes"].as_array().unwrap().iter().filter(|n| n["code"] == "N_NO_PRODUCTION_WRITER").map(|n| n["message"].as_str().unwrap().split(':').next().unwrap().to_owned()).collect::<Vec<_>>()
+    };
+    let cycle = "use identity password\ntable a:\n  principal: principal\n  unique principal\n  read <- allow\n  insert <- exists b[viewer]\n  online only\ntable b:\n  principal: principal\n  unique principal\n  read <- allow\n  insert <- exists a[viewer]\n  online only\nmutation addA(principal: principal):\n  return insert a { principal }\nmutation addB(principal: principal):\n  return insert b { principal }\n";
+    assert_eq!(notes(cycle), ["a", "b"]);
+    assert!(notes(&cycle.replace("insert <- exists a[viewer]", "insert <- exists a[viewer] or viewer = 'service:bootstrap'")).is_empty());
+    let through = "use identity password\ntable parents:\n  owner: principal\n  immutable owner\n  read <- .owner = viewer\n  insert <- .owner = viewer\n  online only\ntable children:\n  parent: parents\n  immutable parent\n  read through .parent\n  insert <- .parent.owner = viewer\n  online only\nmutation addChild(parent: parents):\n  return insert children { parent }\n";

+#[test]
+fn mutation_reply_omits_unrequested_id_and_keeps_result_id() {
+    let project = Project::new("use personas alice\ntable notes:\n  title: text 1..80\n  public\n  insert <- allow\nmutation add(title: text 1..80):\n  return insert notes { title }\nmutation refuse():\n  require false else NO_PROOF\n  return null\n");
+    let (_dev, url) = project.dev(0);

+        assert!(!reply.as_object().unwrap().contains_key("id"), "{reply}");
+        if path.ends_with("add") { assert!(reply["result"]["id"].is_string(), "{reply}"); }
+    }
+    let (_, sent) = http(&url, "POST", "/m/add", Some("alice"), r#"{"id":"proof-write","args":{"title":"second"}}"#);
+    assert_eq!(sent["id"], "proof-write");
+    let (_, replay) = http(&url, "POST", "/m/add", Some("alice"), r#"{"id":"proof-write","args":{"title":"second"}}"#);
+    assert_eq!(replay["replayed"], true); assert!(replay.get("result").is_none());

+        let path = dir.0.join("snapback/schema.q");
+        let original = fs::read_to_string(&path).unwrap();
+        for (identity, expected) in [("use identity password", "the password sign-in"), ("use personas alice", "a persona picker"), ("use identity password", "the password sign-in"), ("use identity", "the guest sign-in")] {
+            let body = original.lines().filter(|line| !line.starts_with("use identity") && !line.starts_with("use personas")).collect::<Vec<_>>().join("\n");
+            fs::write(&path, format!("{identity}\n{body}\n")).unwrap();
+            let out = dir.init(&["--adapt"]); assert!(out.status.success(), "{out:?}");
+            let text = String::from_utf8_lossy(&out.stdout); assert!(text.contains(expected), "{text}");

diff --git a/snapback4/crates/snapback4/tests/witness_web.rs b/snapback4/crates/snapback4/tests/witness_web.rs
new file mode 100644
index 0000000000..ecb3bd9dc7
--- /dev/null
+++ b/snapback4/crates/snapback4/tests/witness_web.rs
@@ -0,0 +1,13 @@
+//! The public binary routes the packaged web helper even outside a project.
+use std::process::Command;
+const BIN: &str = env!("CARGO_BIN_EXE_snapback4");
+#[test]
+fn web_witness_help_and_invalid_input() {
+    let help = Command::new(BIN).args(["witness", "web", "--help"]).output().unwrap();
+    assert!(help.status.success(), "{help:?}");
+    let text = String::from_utf8_lossy(&help.stdout);
+    assert!(text.contains("--mutation") && text.contains("SNAPBACK4_CHROMIUM"));
+    let refused = Command::new(BIN).args(["witness", "web", "--timeout", "zero"]).output().unwrap();
+    assert!(!refused.status.success());
+    assert!(String::from_utf8_lossy(&refused.stderr).contains("E_INPUT"));
+}

// The backend `snapback4 dev` serves. Set VITE_SNAPBACK_URL for a deployment.
export const url = (new URLSearchParams(window.location.search).get("api") ?? import.meta.env.VITE_SNAPBACK_URL ?? "http://127.0.0.1:4400").replace(/\/$/, "");

// A device supplies offline reads for synced tables and queues writes.
// With no synced table, init omits the device and uses the browser write journal.
// localStorageSessionStore() keeps the session across reloads; any script on
// this origin can read it. Device-less browser writes require this store.

import { Platform } from "react-native";
import * as SecureStore from "expo-secure-store";

// Development and production builds carry the native device (offline data and
// a durable outbox); Expo Go has no native module and uses the online client;
// web uses the wasm device.
export const tier = Platform.OS === "web" ? "web" : nativeAvailable() ? "native" : "online";

const sessionStore = {
  get: (key: string) => SecureStore.getItemAsync(key),
  set: (key: string, value: string) => SecureStore.setItemAsync(key, value),
  delete: (key: string) => SecureStore.deleteItemAsync(key),
};
// Browser storage is readable by any script on the page; SecureStore is not.
export const app = createApp({
  url,
  sessionStore: Platform.OS === "web" ? localStorageSessionStore() : sessionStore,
  device: tier === "web" ? webDevice() : tier === "native" ? nativeDevice : undefined,
});

  url,
  persona,
  device: tier === "web" ? webDevice() : tier === "native" ? nativeDevice : undefined,
});
  registerWebWitness(app, api);
  return app;
};
import { useState } from "react";
import { AppProvider, useAuth } from "snapback4/react";
import { app } from "./client.ts";

export function App() { return <AppProvider app={app}><Account /></AppProvider>; }
function Account() {
  const auth = useAuth();
  const [email, setEmail] = useState(""), [password, setPassword] = useState(""), [problem, setProblem] = useState(""), [busy, setBusy] = useState(false);
  if (auth.state === "restoring") return <p>Restoring your session…</p>;
  if (auth.state === "signed-in") return <Todos />;
  async function submit(signup: boolean) {
    setBusy(true); setProblem("");
    try { const result = await (signup ? auth.signup(email, password) : auth.login(email, password)); if (!result.ok) setProblem(result.why.message); }
    catch { setProblem("Sign-in failed; retry."); } finally { setBusy(false); }
  }
  return <main><h1>Sign in</h1><form onSubmit={event => { event.preventDefault(); void submit(false); }}>
    <label>Email<input type="email" autoComplete="username" required value={email} onChange={event => setEmail(event.target.value)} /></label>
    <label>Password<input type="password" autoComplete="current-password" required value={password} onChange={event => setPassword(event.target.value)} /></label>
    <button disabled={busy} type="submit">Sign in</button><button disabled={busy} type="button" onClick={() => void submit(true)}>Sign up</button>
    <p role="alert">{problem || auth.why?.message}</p>
  </form></main>;
}
import { useState } from "react";
import { Button, SafeAreaView, Text, TextInput, View } from "react-native";
import { StatusBar } from "expo-status-bar";
import { AppProvider, useAuth } from "snapback4/react";
import { app } from "./src/client";

export function App() { return <AppProvider app={app}><SafeAreaView style={{ flex: 1 }}><StatusBar style="auto" /><Account /></SafeAreaView></AppProvider>; }
function Account() {
  const auth = useAuth();
  const [email, setEmail] = useState(""), [password, setPassword] = useState(""), [problem, setProblem] = useState(""), [busy, setBusy] = useState(false);
  if (auth.state === "restoring") return <Text>Restoring your session…</Text>;
  if (auth.state === "signed-in") return <Todos />;
  async function submit(signup: boolean) {
    setBusy(true); setProblem("");
    try { const result = await (signup ? auth.signup(email, password) : auth.login(email, password)); if (!result.ok) setProblem(result.why.message); }
    catch { setProblem("Sign-in failed; retry."); } finally { setBusy(false); }
  }
  return <View style={{ padding: 24, gap: 12 }}><Text>Sign in</Text>
    <TextInput accessibilityLabel="Email" autoCapitalize="none" autoComplete="email" keyboardType="email-address" value={email} onChangeText={setEmail} />
    <TextInput accessibilityLabel="Password" secureTextEntry autoComplete="current-password" value={password} onChangeText={setPassword} />
    <Button title="Sign in" disabled={busy} onPress={() => void submit(false)} /><Button title="Sign up" disabled={busy} onPress={() => void submit(true)} />
    <Text accessibilityRole="alert">{problem || auth.why?.message}</Text>
  </View>;
}

//! Starter leftovers on the init-first path: `init --web` (or `--expo`) wrote
//! the todo starter, then the project replaced `snapback/schema.q`. The
//! starter's own files that still match the bytes init wrote, but no longer
//! fit the schema, are leftovers: `check` names them and `init --adapt`
//! removes or rewrites them. A file the project changed is never touched.
// @ref LLP 3000.011.003.000#7.2 — leftovers are judged by the starter's original bytes, never by name alone.
use super::starter::{self, Body, Platform};
use super::{OwnSchema, own_schema};
use std::path::Path;

/// One unmodified starter file that no longer fits the project's schema.
pub(crate) struct Leftover {
    pub path: &'static str,
    pub why: String,
    /// The text `init --adapt` writes; `None` removes the file.
    pub rewrite: Option<String>,
}

/// Starter operations its fixtures, cases and `Todos` screen call.
const STARTER_OPERATIONS: [&str; 4] = ["list", "add", "toggle", "remove"];

/// Whether a schema still has the starter's `todos` table and the operations
/// its fixtures, cases and `Todos` screen call.
pub(super) fn fits_todos(own: &OwnSchema) -> bool {
    own.tables.iter().any(|table| table == "todos")
        && STARTER_OPERATIONS.iter().all(|op| own.operations.iter().any(|name| name == op))
}

/// What the schema in `dir` leaves of the starter, and the advice that goes
/// with it (a sign-in the project must write by hand). No schema: nothing.
pub(crate) fn leftovers(dir: &Path) -> (Vec<Leftover>, Vec<String>) {
    let Ok(Some(own)) = own_schema(dir) else { return (Vec::new(), Vec::new()) };
    let mut found = Vec::new();
    let mut advice = Vec::new();
    let todos = fits_todos(&own);
    if !todos {
        for (path, text) in starter::FIXTURES {
            if read(dir, path).is_some_and(|current| current == super::without_design_references(text)) {
                let what = if path.ends_with("todos.jsonl") { "rows for the todo starter's `todos` table" } else { "cases for the todo starter's list/add/toggle/remove" };
                found.push(Leftover { path, why: format!("the unmodified {what}; your snapback/*.q has no todos table with those operations, so `snapback4 test` would refuse it"), rewrite: None });
            }
        }
    }
    for platform in [Platform::Web, Platform::Expo] {
        screens(dir, platform, &own, todos, &mut found, &mut advice);
    }
    readme(dir, &own, todos, &mut found);
    app_json(dir, &mut found);
    (found, advice)
}

/// The starter README, while it is still one init wrote (for either screen,
/// at the ports it names), when it describes another screen than the schema
/// now gets: the Todos README beside a project's schema, or the reverse.
// @ref LLP 3000.011.003.000#7.4 — wave 6 (042): adapt rewrote the screen but left a README about todos and guest sign-in.
fn readme(dir: &Path, own: &OwnSchema, todos: bool, found: &mut Vec<Leftover>) {
    let platform = if dir.join("src/App.tsx").is_file() { Platform::Web } else if dir.join("App.tsx").is_file() { Platform::Expo } else { return };
    let title = super::title(dir);
    let personas = own.identity.is_none() && !own.personas.is_empty();
    let wanted_body = if todos { Body::Todos } else { Body::Home(None) };
    for path in ["README.md", "README.snapback.md"] {
        let Some(text) = read(dir, path) else { continue };
        let port = number_after(&text, "127.0.0.1:");
        let web_port = number_after(&text, "localhost:");
        let ports = [(port, web_port), (None, web_port), (port, None), (None, None)];
        // The offline paragraph names the schema's synced tables when init
        // wrote it; any of them (or 0.4.22's unconditional claim) still matches.
        let masked = starter::without_offline(&text);
        let matched = ports.into_iter().find(|&(port, web_port)| {
            [(Body::Todos, false), (Body::Home(None), false), (Body::Home(None), true)].iter()
                .any(|(body, personas)| [false, true].iter().any(|password| masked == starter::without_offline(&super::localize(path, &super::without_design_references(&starter::identity_readme(platform, &title, body, *personas, &starter::Offline::Unknown, *password)), port, web_port))))
        });
        let Some((port, web_port)) = matched else { continue };
        let wanted = super::localize(path, &super::without_design_references(&starter::identity_readme(platform, &title, &wanted_body, personas, &own.offline, own.identity.is_some_and(|(password, guests)| password && !guests))), port, web_port);
        if wanted == text { continue; }
        let about = if todos { "the todo starter's operations, which your snapback/*.q declares again" } else { "your snapback/*.q and the Home screen, named after this directory" };
        let only_offline = !text.starts_with("# Todos") && starter::without_offline(&wanted) == masked;
        // A schema that does not compile right now says nothing about what it syncs.
        if only_offline && own.offline == starter::Offline::Unknown { continue; }
        let why = if only_offline {
            "the unmodified starter README says what works offline for another schema; adapt rewrites that paragraph from the tables your snapback/*.q syncs".to_owned()
        } else {
            format!("the unmodified starter README describes {}; adapt rewrites it to describe {about}", if text.starts_with("# Todos") { "the todo starter (its todos table and guest sign-in)" } else { "the Home screen for another schema" })
        };
        found.push(Leftover { path, why, rewrite: Some(wanted) });
    }
}

/// An Expo `app.json` that still carries the todo starter's slug, scheme and
/// bundle ids (written by 0.4.21 and earlier): adapt names them after the
/// directory and changes nothing else in the file.
// @ref LLP 3000.011.003.000#7.4 — wave 6 (067): adapted Expo apps kept dev.snapback4.todos.
fn app_json(dir: &Path, found: &mut Vec<Leftover>) {
    let Some(text) = read(dir, "app.json") else { return };
    let Ok(config) = serde_json::from_str::<serde_json::Value>(&text) else { return };
    let expo = &config["expo"];
    let [(_, slug), (_, scheme), (_, bundle)] = starter::TODO_IDS;
    if expo["slug"] != slug || expo["scheme"] != scheme || expo["ios"]["bundleIdentifier"] != bundle || expo["android"]["package"] != bundle { return; }
    let (new_slug, new_scheme, new_bundle) = starter::app_ids(&super::title(dir));
    let quoted = |value: &str| format!("\"{value}\"");
    let rewrite = text.replace(&quoted(bundle), &quoted(&new_bundle)).replace(&quoted(slug), &quoted(&new_slug)).replace(&quoted(scheme), &quoted(&new_scheme));
    found.push(Leftover { path: "app.json", why: format!("the todo starter's ids (slug {slug}, scheme {scheme}, bundle id {bundle}); adapt names them after this directory ({new_slug}, {new_scheme}, {new_bundle})"), rewrite: Some(rewrite) });
}

fn number_after(text: &str, prefix: &str) -> Option<u16> {
    let at = text.find(prefix)? + prefix.len();
    let digits: String = text[at..].chars().take_while(char::is_ascii_digit).collect();
    digits.parse().ok()
}

/// The sign-in a schema admits, as the starter screen it needs: the guest
/// screen (`None`), the persona picker (`Some(personas)`), or no starter
/// screen fits (`Err(advice)`).
#[derive(Clone, PartialEq, Eq)]
enum SignIn { Guest, Password, Personas(Vec<String>) }
impl SignIn {
    fn label(&self) -> String {
        match self { Self::Guest => "the guest sign-in".into(), Self::Password => "the password sign-in".into(), Self::Personas(names) => format!("a persona picker ({})", names.join(", ")) }
    }
    fn personas(&self) -> Option<&[String]> { match self { Self::Personas(names) => Some(names), _ => None } }
}
fn wanted(own: &OwnSchema) -> Result<SignIn, &'static str> {
    match own.identity {
        Some((_, true)) => Ok(SignIn::Guest),
        Some((true, false)) => Ok(SignIn::Password),
        None if !own.personas.is_empty() => Ok(SignIn::Personas(own.personas.clone())),
        _ => Err("declares neither `use identity` nor `use personas`, so nobody can act: add `use identity` for the guest sign-in or `use personas alice, bob` for development, then rerun `snapback4 init --adapt`"),
    }
}

fn screens(dir: &Path, platform: Platform, own: &OwnSchema, todos: bool, found: &mut Vec<Leftover>, advice: &mut Vec<String>) {
    let (app_path, client_path) = platform.paths();
    let (Some(app), Some(client)) = (read(dir, app_path), read(dir, client_path)) else { return };
    // Which starter screen is on disk, if both files are still as init wrote
    // them: its sign-in (guest, or a persona picker) and its body.
    let journal_shape = starter::Offline::Tables { synced: vec![], total: 0 };
    let client_shape = normalized(&client);
    let matches_client = |reference: &str| client_shape == normalized(reference)
        || client_shape == normalized(&starter::web_client(reference.to_owned(), &journal_shape));
    let current = [Body::Todos, Body::Home(first_read(&app))].into_iter().find_map(|body| {
        let (guest_app, guest_client) = starter::screens(platform, None, &body);
        let (persona_app, persona_client) = starter::screens(platform, Some(&[]), &body);
        let (password_app, password_client) = starter::identity_screens(platform, None, &body, true);
        if app == guest_app && matches_client(&guest_client) { Some((SignIn::Guest, body)) }
        else if app == password_app && matches_client(&password_client) { Some((SignIn::Password, body)) }
        else if app == persona_app && matches_client(&persona_client) { Some((SignIn::Personas(personas_of(&client)), body)) }
        else { None }
    });
    let Some((sign_in, body)) = current else { return };
    // A Home keeps its read while the schema still declares that query
    // argument-free; a Home without one stays as written (it fits any schema).
    let wanted_body = match &body {
        _ if todos => Body::Todos,
        Body::Home(None) => Body::Home(None),
        Body::Home(Some(op)) if own.reads.contains(op) => body.clone(),
        _ => Body::Home(own.reads.first().cloned()),
    };
    // A sign-in no starter screen fits keeps the one on disk, with advice.
    let wanted_sign_in = match wanted(own) {
        Ok(wanted) => wanted,
        Err(why) => {
            advice.push(format!("{app_path} is the starter's {}, but your snapback/*.q {why}", sign_in.label()));
            sign_in.clone()
        }
    };
    let (app_text, mut client_text) = starter::identity_screens(platform, wanted_sign_in.personas(), &wanted_body, wanted_sign_in == SignIn::Password);
    client_text = starter::web_client(client_text, &own.offline);
    let client_changed = client_shape != normalized(&client_text);
    if wanted_sign_in == sign_in && app == app_text && wanted_body == body && !client_changed { return; }
    let mut reasons = Vec::new();
    let mut client_leftover = None;
    if wanted_sign_in != sign_in {
        let (from, to) = (sign_in.label(), wanted_sign_in.label());
        let because = match &wanted_sign_in { SignIn::Personas(_) => "your snapback/*.q declares `use personas` and no `use identity`", SignIn::Password => "your snapback/*.q declares `use identity password`", SignIn::Guest => "your snapback/*.q admits guests" };
        reasons.push(format!("the unmodified starter screen shows {from}, but {because}; adapt writes {to}"));
        // Keep the backend port init wrote.
        if let Some(port) = port_of(&client) { client_text = client_text.replace("127.0.0.1:4400", &format!("127.0.0.1:{port}")); }
        client_leftover = Some(Leftover { path: client_path, why: format!("the unmodified starter client for {from}; adapt writes {to}"), rewrite: Some(client_text.clone()) });
    }
    if wanted_sign_in == sign_in && client_changed {
        if let Some(port) = port_of(&client) { client_text = client_text.replace("127.0.0.1:4400", &format!("127.0.0.1:{port}")); }
        let why = "the unmodified web client needs the device or principal journal selected by your schema's synced tables".to_owned();
        reasons.push(why.clone());
        client_leftover = Some(Leftover { path: client_path, why, rewrite: Some(client_text.clone()) });
    }
    if wanted_body != body {
        reasons.push(match (&wanted_body, &body) {
            (Body::Home(_), Body::Home(Some(op))) => format!("its Home screen reads api.{op}, which your snapback/*.q no longer declares as a query without required arguments; adapt writes the Home screen again{}", match &wanted_body { Body::Home(Some(next)) => format!(", reading api.{next}"), _ => String::new() }),
            (Body::Home(_), _) => "its Todos screen calls the starter's list/add/toggle/remove, which your snapback/*.q does not declare, so it would not typecheck; adapt writes a Home screen that compiles against any schema (the signed-in viewer, your operations from snapback/generated/api.ts, and your first query without required arguments rendered with matchRead) for you to replace".to_owned(),
            (Body::Todos, _) => "your snapback/*.q declares the todo starter's operations again; adapt writes the Todos screen back".to_owned(),
        });
    }
    let why = reasons.join("; ");
    let why = if why.starts_with("the unmodified") { why } else { format!("the unmodified starter screen: {why}") };
    found.push(Leftover { path: app_path, why, rewrite: Some(app_text) });
    found.extend(client_leftover);
}

/// The `api` path a starter Home's first read calls, if it has one.
fn first_read(app: &str) -> Option<String> {
    let after = app.split_once("function FirstRead()")?.1;
    let op = after.split_once("useQuery(api.")?.1.split_once(',')?.0;
    Some(op.to_owned())
}

fn read(dir: &Path, path: &str) -> Option<String> {
    let path = dir.join(path);
    path.symlink_metadata().ok().filter(|metadata| metadata.is_file())?;
    std::fs::read_to_string(path).ok()
}

/// A starter client without what init or the schema varies: the backend port
/// and the persona list.
fn normalized(client: &str) -> String {
    let lines = client.split_inclusive('\n').map(|line| {
        if line.starts_with(PERSONAS) { format!("{PERSONAS}[];\n") } else { line.to_owned() }
    }).collect::<String>();
    match port_of(&lines) { Some(port) => lines.replace(&format!("127.0.0.1:{port}"), "127.0.0.1:4400"), None => lines }
}

const PERSONAS: &str = "export const personas: readonly string[] = ";

fn personas_of(client: &str) -> Vec<String> {
    client.lines().find_map(|line| line.strip_prefix(PERSONAS))
        .and_then(|list| serde_json::from_str(list.trim_end().trim_end_matches(';')).ok()).unwrap_or_default()
}

fn port_of(client: &str) -> Option<&str> {
    let at = client.find("127.0.0.1:")? + "127.0.0.1:".len();
    let digits = client[at..].find(|c: char| !c.is_ascii_digit()).unwrap_or(client.len() - at);
    (digits > 0).then(|| &client[at..at + digits])
}

/// One `check` line, or none: the leftovers and the command that fixes them.
pub(crate) fn report(dir: &Path) -> Option<String> {
    let (found, advice) = leftovers(dir);
    if found.is_empty() && advice.is_empty() { return None; }
    let mut lines = Vec::new();
    for leftover in &found { lines.push(format!("  {}: {}", leftover.path, leftover.why)); }
    for line in &advice { lines.push(format!("  {line}")); }
    if !found.is_empty() {
        lines.push("  fix: snapback4 init --adapt (removes or rewrites only these files, which still match the starter; your own files are never touched)".into());
    }
    Some(format!("starter: {} left from `snapback4 init` that {} not fit your schema\n{}",
        if found.is_empty() { "a screen".to_owned() } else { format!("{} file{}", found.len(), if found.len() == 1 { "" } else { "s" }) },
        if found.len() == 1 || found.is_empty() { "does" } else { "do" }, lines.join("\n")))
}

/// The same, for `check --json`: `null` when nothing is left over.
pub(crate) fn report_json(dir: &Path) -> serde_json::Value {
    let (found, advice) = leftovers(dir);
    if found.is_empty() && advice.is_empty() { return serde_json::Value::Null; }
    serde_json::json!({
        "files": found.iter().map(|leftover| serde_json::json!({ "path": leftover.path, "why": leftover.why, "adapt": if leftover.rewrite.is_some() { "rewrite" } else { "remove" } })).collect::<Vec<_>>(),
        "advice": advice,
        "fix": if found.is_empty() { serde_json::Value::Null } else { "snapback4 init --adapt".into() },
    })
}

+
+Beside the persona form (`guide personas`), use this for `use identity password`:
+```tsx
+import { useState } from "react";

+  if (auth.state === "restoring") return <>Restoring…</>;
+  if (auth.state === "signed-in") return <>Signed in as {auth.viewer}</>;
+  const submit = async (signup: boolean) => { const r = await (signup ? auth.signup(email, password) : auth.login(email, password)); if (!r.ok) setWhy(r.why.message); };
+  return <><input aria-label="Email" value={email} onChange={e => setEmail(e.target.value)} /><input aria-label="Password" type="password" value={password} onChange={e => setPassword(e.target.value)} />

+SDK: `{ok:true, session}` / `{ok:false, why}`; wire: `{session}` / `{denied}`. Expo imports `Platform` and `* as SecureStore`:
+`sessionStore: Platform.OS === "web" ? localStorageSessionStore() : { get: SecureStore.getItemAsync, set: SecureStore.setItemAsync, delete: SecureStore.deleteItemAsync }`.
+Synced web: `webDevice()`; native: `nativeDevice`; no-sync web: journal. `init --web`/`--expo` writes the form. REL owns the deploy checklist.

+
+An empty complete list is `absent` only with `cut:null`, no continuation or blocking prediction. List props: `Read<T[]>` (`T extends { id: string }`); operation props: `UseQuery<typeof api.op>`.
